### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP と UDP の一番の違いは、**TCP は届いたことと順番を保証し、UDP はそれをしない（速さを優先する）**ことです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales y actualizar tu rama con la base antes de compartirla, siempre que nadie más esté trabajando sobre ella. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o compartida, porque reescribir su historial obligaría a los demás a arreglar sus copias.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是有连接、可靠的，保证数据按序、不丢不重地送到；UDP 是无连接、不保证的，发了就不管，换来的是更快、更轻的传输。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you're cleaning up your own unshared branch before a PR, so history reads as a clean sequence of changes. Merge when the branch is shared or already public, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have built on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接指定就行：

```
bun test test/user.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，bun 会按路径里的片段匹配：

```
bun test user
```

如果只想跑文件里的某一个用例，加 `-t` 按用例名过滤：

```
bun test user.test.ts -t "登录"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data files. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart so committed changes survive and half-finished ones can be rolled back.
```

```

要做的：
- 找到 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁的范围缩小（只锁真正需要保护的数据，不要整段或整个结构加锁）。
- 改动留在当前分支上提交，不要合并到主分支，不要推送，不要改动 PR 的状态（它现在是 draft）。
- 跑一遍相关测试，把结果（通过/失败的数量）和改了哪几处作为报告给我。
- 如果 review 里的锁粒度意见有两种理解、拿不准，先停下来问我，不要猜。

完成标准：锁范围已缩小，相关测试跑过且结果附在报告里，改动已提交在分支上。
```

```

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with nights 2 and 3 so far, and it fills in after each night's run.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2072 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latestSub">Waiting for the first run</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Write benchmark, by night</h2>
  <div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
(function(){
  function fmtRate(n){ return n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n/1000) + 'k/s' : n + '/s'; }
  function el(tag, cls, text){
    var e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = String(text);
    return e;
  }
  async function render(){
    var d = await ducter.get('nights');
    var nights = Array.isArray(d) ? d.slice() : [];
    nights.sort(function(a,b){ return b.night - a.night; });
    var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
    var latestSub = document.getElementById('latestSub');
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.textContent = '';
    if (!nights.length){
      latest.textContent = '—';
      latest.className = 'k-sum-value k-none';
      latestSub.textContent = 'Waiting for the first run';
      list.appendChild(el('div', 'k-empty', 'Nothing yet. Each night’s result lands here.'));
      return;
    }
    var top = nights[0];
    latest.className = 'k-sum-value';
    latest.textContent = fmtRate(top.writes);
    var sub = 'Night ' + top.night + ' · p99 ' + top.p99 + ' ms';
    if (top.stalls != null) sub += ' · ' + top.stalls + ' compaction stalls';
    latestSub.textContent = sub;
    nights.forEach(function(n){
      var row = el('div', 'k-row');
      var main = el('div', 'k-row-main', 'Night ' + n.night);
      var s = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' ms';
      if (n.stalls != null) s += ' · ' + n.stalls + ' compaction stalls';
      var rowSub = el('div', 'k-row-sub', s);
      var val = el('div', 'k-row-value', fmtRate(n.writes));
      row.appendChild(main); row.appendChild(rowSub); row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4},{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}
```

```
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const sum = xs.reduce((a, x) => a + (Number(x.yuan) || 0), 0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent = "¥" + sum;
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔" : "还没记";
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  if (!xs.length) {
    const e = document.createElement("div");
    e.className = "k-empty";
    e.textContent = "还没记";
    list.appendChild(e);
    return;
  }
  for (const x of xs) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length + " 笔,合计 ¥" + sum);
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
```

```
<script>
const CATS = ['交通','住宿','餐饮','其他'];
let cat = '交通';
let openId = null;
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));}
function items(){const v = ducter.get('items'); return Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];}
function fmt(n){return '¥' + Number(n).toLocaleString('zh-CN');}
function saveItems(list){ducter.set('items', list);}
function render(){
  const list = items();
  const total = list.reduce((s,i)=>s + Number(i.amount||0), 0);
  const tv = document.get …  data-cat="' + c + '" aria-pressed="' + (c===cat) + '">' + c + '</button>'
  ).join('');
}
document.getElementById('cats').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const b = e.target.closest('[data-cat]'); if(!b) return;
  cat = b.dataset.cat; renderCats();
});
document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if(del){
    const id = Number(del.dataset.del);
    saveItems(items().filter(i => i.id !== id));
    openId = null; return;
  }
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row'); if(!row) return;
  const id = Number(row.dataset.id);
  openId = openId === id ? null : id;
  render();
});
document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('amt').value);
  if(!isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) return;
  const name = document.getElementById('name').value.trim() || cat;
  const d = new Date();
  const date = (d.getMonth()+1) + '/' + d.getDate();
  saveItems(items().concat([{id: Date.now(), name, cat, amount, date}]));
  document.getElementById('name').value = '';
  document.getElementById('amt').value = '';
});
ducter.onChange(render);
renderCats();
render();
```

```
      amt: Math.round(amt * 100) / 100,
      cat: cat,
      note: $('note').value.trim(),
      date: ymd(new Date())
    });
    $('amt').value = '';
    $('note').value = '';
    save();
    render();
  };

  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('expenses')).then(function (v) {
      items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }

  ducter.onChange(load);
  renderCats();
  load();
</script>
; its data now (what it opens with): {}
```

```
  const t = $('time').value.trim();

  if (editing) {
    const f = feeds.find(x => x.id === editing);
    if (f) { f.t = t; f.ml = ml; }
  } else {
    feeds.push({ id: Date.now(), d: today(), t: t, ml: ml });
  }
  await ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
  render();
  resetForm();
};

$('del').onclick = async () => {
  feeds = feeds.filter(x => x.id !== editing);
  await ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
  render();
  resetForm();
};

ducter.onChange(async () => {
  const v = await ducter.get('feeds');
  feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
});

(async () => {

  feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  $('time').value = nowHM();
  render();
})();
```

```
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("en-US"):"还没记");
```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一台米白色的卡通笔记本电脑，3D 黏土风格，屏幕是空白的，黑色边框，键盘有立体感。

适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕本身就表示"这里还没有内容"，很贴切，比如没有文档、没有记录、没有设备时都能用。它颜色柔和、造型简单，不会让人觉得出错了，还带点亲切感。

用的时候注意两点：一是尺寸别太大，大约 160 到 200 像素比较合适，太大会抢走下面的文字和按钮；二是深色模式下，米白色会比较亮，深蓝色边框可能和深色背景融在一起，最好单独看一下效果。
```

```
要求：

- 明确断言：到期时刻（now == 写入时间 + TTL）该值已过期（取不到）；到期前一刻（差 1 个单位）仍然有效。边界的具体语义以代码现有约定为准，如果现有实现和这个约定不一致，先在报告里说明，不要擅自改实现。
- 测试继续用假时钟，不用真实 sleep 或真实计时。
- 完成标准：跑全部测试通过，并把测试输出（通过数）和新测试的断言内容写进报告；连跑 20 次都稳定通过。
```
